454                     //------------------------------------------------------
455                     //  生成tag_name_index值
456                     //------------------------------------------------------
457                     wire [SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP-1:0] tag_name_index_hot;
458                     wire [INDEX_WITH-1:0] tag_name_index;
459                     reg  [INDEX_WITH-1:0] tag_name_index_d;
460                     //独热码转二进制码
461                     wire [INDEX_WITH-1 : 0] tag_name_index_hot2bin_temp1 [SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP-1 : 0];
462                     wire [SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP-1 : 0] tag_name_index_hot2bin_temp2 [INDEX_WITH-1 : 0];
463                     //生成独热码
464                     generate 
465                         for (i = 0 ; i < SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP ; i = i + 1) begin 
466                             assign tag_name_index_hot[i] = {rspt2rspo_axid , rspt2rspo_opc , 1'b1} == tag_name[i] ? 1'b1 : 1'b0;
467                         end
468                     endgenerate
469                     //独热码转二进制码
470                     generate
471                     	for(i = 0; i < SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP; i = i+1)begin 
472                     		assign tag_name_index_hot2bin_temp1[i] = tag_name_index_hot[i]? i:'b0;
473                     	end
474                     endgenerate
475                     generate
476                     	for(i = 0; i < SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP; i = i+1)begin 
477                     		for(j = 0; j < INDEX_WITH; j = j+1) begin  
478                     			assign tag_name_index_hot2bin_temp2[j][i] = tag_name_index_hot2bin_temp1[i][j];
479                     		end
480                     	end
481                     endgenerate
482                     generate
483                     	for(j = 0; j < INDEX_WITH; j = j+1)begin 
484                     		assign tag_name_index[j] = |tag_name_index_hot2bin_temp2[j];
485                     	end
486                     endgenerate
487                     
488                     // 保持当前常规响应所对应的tag_name索引。多拍响应仅在HEAD拍提供
489                     // 新的响应类型，LW拍必须使用HEAD拍锁存的索引推进tag计数器。
490                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
491        1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0)
492        1/1                  tag_name_index_d <= #DLY 'd0;
493        1/1              else if(rspt2rspo_head == 1'b1 && rspt2rspo_valid == 1'b1 &&
494                                 rspo2rspt_ready == 1'b1)
495        1/1                  tag_name_index_d <= #DLY tag_name_index;
                        MISSING_ELSE
496                     end
497                     
498                     wire [INDEX_WITH-1:0] norm_rsp_tag_name_index;
499                     assign norm_rsp_tag_name_index =
500                         (rspt2rspo_head == 1'b1) ? tag_name_index : tag_name_index_d;
501                     
502                     //------------------------------------------------------
503                     //  生成当前特殊响应对应的tag_name索引
504                     //------------------------------------------------------
505                     reg [INDEX_WITH-1:0] spec_tag_name_index;
506                     reg [INDEX_WITH-1:0] wd_tag_name_index;
507                     integer tag_lookup_i;
508                     always @(*) begin
509        1/1              spec_tag_name_index = 'd0;
510        1/1              wd_tag_name_index = 'd0;
511        1/1              for(tag_lookup_i=0; tag_lookup_i<SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP;
512                             tag_lookup_i=tag_lookup_i+1) begin
513        1/1                  if({spec_req_buffer[buff_index][BUFF_OPC_OFFSET +: AXID_WITH+2],
514                                 1'b1} == tag_name[tag_lookup_i])
515        1/1                      spec_tag_name_index = tag_lookup_i;
                        MISSING_ELSE
516        1/1                  if({wd2rspo_axid, wd2rspo_opc, 1'b1} == tag_name[tag_lookup_i])
517        1/1                      wd_tag_name_index = tag_lookup_i;
                        MISSING_ELSE
518                         end
519                     end
520                     
521                     //------------------------------------------------------
522                     //  tag_cnt自增操作
523                     //------------------------------------------------------
524                     reg [$clog2(SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP)-1:0] tag_cnt [SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP-1:0];
525                     reg [INDEX_WITH-1:0] spec_rsp_tag_name_index;
526                     reg [AXID_WITH-1:0] spec_rsp_axid;
527                     reg [1:0] spec_rsp_opc;
528                     reg [2:0] spec_rsp_errcode;
529                     reg [1:0] spec_rsp_status;
530                     
531                     integer b;
532                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
533        1/1              if (resetn == 1'b0) begin
534        1/1                  for (b = 0; b < SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP; b = b + 1)
535        1/1                      tag_cnt[b] <= #DLY 'd0;
536                         // A bufferable write already advanced this ordering tag when its early
537                         // response was dispatched.  Its later real B response only retires the
538                         // request/watchdog state and must not advance the same tag a second time.
539        1/1              end else if(rspt2rspo_valid == 1'b1 &&
540                                     rspt2rspo_lw == 1'b1 &&
541                                     rspo2rspt_ready == 1'b1 &&
542                                     buff_rsp_flag == 1'b0) begin
543        1/1                  if(tag_cnt[norm_rsp_tag_name_index] == SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP-1)
544        1/1                      tag_cnt[norm_rsp_tag_name_index] <= #DLY 'd0;
545                             else
546        1/1                      tag_cnt[norm_rsp_tag_name_index] <= #DLY
547                                     tag_cnt[norm_rsp_tag_name_index] + 1'b1;
548        1/1              end else if(spec_dispatch_en == 1'b1 &&
549                                     timout_fifo_rden == 1'b0) begin// 启动特殊响应时推进对应类型tag，超时响应不推进
550        1/1                  if(tag_cnt[spec_tag_name_index] == SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP-1)
551        1/1                      tag_cnt[spec_tag_name_index] <= #DLY 'd0;
552                             else
553        1/1                      tag_cnt[spec_tag_name_index] <= #DLY
554                                     tag_cnt[spec_tag_name_index] + 1'b1;
555                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
556                     end
557                     
558                     //------------------------------------------------------
559                     //  对全部特殊请求逐项判断是否已经到达各自类型的响应顺序头
560                     //------------------------------------------------------
561                     integer spec_ready_i;
562                     integer spec_ready_tag_i;
563                     always @(*) begin
564        1/1              spec_req_ready_hot = 'd0;
565        1/1              for(spec_ready_i=0;
566                             spec_ready_i<SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP;
567                             spec_ready_i=spec_ready_i+1) begin
568        1/1                  if(spec_req_buffer[spec_ready_i][0] == 1'b1) begin
569                                 // fir_flag用于新类型首次分配时的快速路径。
570        1/1                      if(spec_req_buffer[spec_ready_i][BUFF_FIR_FLAG_OFFSET] == 1'b1)
571        1/1                          spec_req_ready_hot[spec_ready_i] = 1'b1;
572                                 else begin
573                                     // follower必须和该条目自己的{AXID,OPC,TAG}比较，
574                                     // 不能只依赖最后一笔普通响应的类型。
575        1/1                          for(spec_ready_tag_i=0;
576                                         spec_ready_tag_i<SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP;
577                                         spec_ready_tag_i=spec_ready_tag_i+1) begin
578        1/1                              if(tag_name[spec_ready_tag_i][0] == 1'b1 &&
579                                            tag_name[spec_ready_tag_i][1 +: AXID_WITH+2] ==
580                                                spec_req_buffer[spec_ready_i]
581                                                    [BUFF_OPC_OFFSET +: AXID_WITH+2] &&
582                                            tag_cnt[spec_ready_tag_i] ==
583                                                spec_req_buffer[spec_ready_i]
584                                                    [BUFF_TAGCNT_OFFSET +: TAG_CNT_WITH])
585        1/1                                  spec_req_ready_hot[spec_ready_i] = 1'b1;
                        MISSING_ELSE
586                                     end
587                                 end
588                             end
                        MISSING_ELSE
589                         end
590                     end
591                     
592                     assign spec_req_ready_en = |spec_req_ready_hot;
593                     
594                     integer spec_ready_sel_i;
595                     always @(*) begin
596        1/1              spec_req_ready_index = 'd0;
597                         // 从高到低覆盖，最终保留最低有效下标。
598        1/1              for(spec_ready_sel_i=SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP-1;
599                             spec_ready_sel_i>=0;
600                             spec_ready_sel_i=spec_ready_sel_i-1)
601        1/1                  if(spec_req_ready_hot[spec_ready_sel_i] == 1'b1)
602        1/1                      spec_req_ready_index = spec_ready_sel_i;
                        MISSING_ELSE
603                     end
604                     
605                     
606                     
607                     //------------------------------------------------------
608                     //  接收由rsp_trans发送的响应信息
609                     //------------------------------------------------------
610                     reg [AXID_WITH-1:0]  rsp_axid;
611                     reg [AUSER_WITH-1:0] rsp_user;
612                     reg [1:0]            rsp_opc;
613                     reg [2:0]            rsp_errcode;
614                     reg [1:0]            rsp_status;
615                     reg [9*NBYTEPERWORD:0] rsp_data;
616                     
617                     // Keep the complete req_order lookup key for the current normal response
618                     // packet.  rsp_trans only guarantees a new AXID/OPC on HEAD; body flits must
619                     // continue to update the same head-buffer entry even when another AXID is
620                     // interleaved afterwards.
621                     wire [AXID_WITH+TAG_CNT_WITH+1:0] norm_rsp_head_index;
622                     assign norm_rsp_head_index =
623                         (rspt2rspo_head == 1'b1) ?
624                             {rspt2rspo_axid, rspt2rspo_opc, tag_cnt[tag_name_index]} :
625                             {rsp_axid,       rsp_opc,       tag_cnt[tag_name_index_d]};
626                     
627                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
628        1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
629        1/1                  rsp_axid <= #DLY 'd0;
630        1/1                  rsp_user <= #DLY 'd0;
631        1/1                  rsp_opc  <= #DLY 'd0;
632        1/1                  rsp_errcode <= #DLY 'd0;
633        1/1                  rsp_status <= #DLY OK;
634        1/1              end else if(rspt2rspo_head == 1'b1 && rspt2rspo_valid == 1'b1 &&
635                                     rspo2rspt_ready == 1'b1)begin
636        1/1                  rsp_axid <= #DLY rspt2rspo_axid;
637        1/1                  rsp_user <= #DLY rspt2rspo_auser;
638        1/1                  rsp_opc  <= #DLY rspt2rspo_opc;
639        1/1                  rsp_errcode <= #DLY rspt2rspo_errcode;
640        1/1                  rsp_status <= #DLY rspt2rspo_status;
641                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
642                     end
643                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
644        1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
645        1/1                  rsp_data <= #DLY 'd0;
646        1/1              end else if(rspt2rspo_valid == 1'b1 && rspo2rspt_ready == 1'b1)begin
647        1/1                  rsp_data <= #DLY rspt2rspo_data;
648                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
649                     end
650                     
651                     //------------------------------------------------------
652                     //  生成follo_req_buff_index值
653                     //------------------------------------------------------
654                     wire [SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP-1:0] follo_req_buff_index_hot;
655                     wire [INDEX_WITH-1:0] follo_req_buff_index;
656                     //独热码转二进制码
657                     wire [INDEX_WITH-1 : 0] follo_req_buff_index_hot2bin_temp1 [SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP-1 : 0];
658                     wire [SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP-1 : 0] follo_req_buff_index_hot2bin_temp2 [INDEX_WITH-1 : 0];
659                     //生成独热码
660                     generate
661                         for (i = 0 ; i < SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP ; i = i + 1) begin
662                             assign follo_req_buff_index_hot[i] =
663                                 ({((cur_state == SPEC_RSP) ? spec_rsp_axid : rsp_axid),
664                                   ((cur_state == SPEC_RSP) ? spec_rsp_opc  : rsp_opc)}
665                                      == spec_req_buffer[i][BUFF_OPC_OFFSET +: 2+AXID_WITH] &&
666                                  spec_req_buffer[i][BUFF_TAGCNT_OFFSET +: TAG_CNT_WITH]
667                                      == tag_cnt[(cur_state == SPEC_RSP) ?
668                                                 spec_rsp_tag_name_index : tag_name_index_d] &&
669                                  spec_req_buffer[i][0] == 1'b1)
670                                 ? 1'b1 : 1'b0;
671                         end
672                     endgenerate
673                     //独热码转二进制码
674                     generate
675                     	for(i = 0; i < SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP; i = i+1)begin 
676                     		assign follo_req_buff_index_hot2bin_temp1[i] = follo_req_buff_index_hot[i]? i:'b0;
677                     	end
678                     endgenerate
679                     generate
680                     	for(i = 0; i < SPEC_REQ_BUFF_DEEP; i = i+1)begin 
681                     		for(j = 0; j < INDEX_WITH; j = j+1) begin  
682                     			assign follo_req_buff_index_hot2bin_temp2[j][i] = follo_req_buff_index_hot2bin_temp1[i][j];
683                     		end
684                     	end
685                     endgenerate
686                     generate
687                     	for(j = 0; j < INDEX_WITH; j = j+1)begin 
688                     		assign follo_req_buff_index[j] = |follo_req_buff_index_hot2bin_temp2[j];
689                     	end
690                     endgenerate
691                     
692                     
693                     //------------------------------------------------------
694                     //  判断该响应对应请求的下一笔请求是否为ERR
695                     //------------------------------------------------------
696                     
697                     assign follo_err_en = (follo_req_buff_index_hot !='d0) ? 1'b1 : 1'b0;
698                     
699                     
700                     
701                     
702                     
703                     
704                     //------------------------------------------------------
705                     //  生成特殊请求自组包的body部分
706                     //------------------------------------------------------
707                     // reqo2rspo_rsp_len is the payload byte count minus one. Calculate all
708                     // positions relative to the first aligned response flit so that byte lanes
709                     // are never compared directly with a flit index.
710                     wire [LEN_WITH:0] first_valid_lane;
711                     wire [LEN_WITH:0] last_byte_offset;
712                     wire [LEN_WITH:0] last_valid_lane;
713                     wire [LEN_WITH:0] total_flit;       // Last flit index, starting from zero
714                     
715                     assign first_valid_lane = reqo2rspo_rsp_addr & (NBYTEPERWORD-1);
716                     assign last_byte_offset = first_valid_lane + {1'b0, reqo2rspo_rsp_len};
717                     assign last_valid_lane  = last_byte_offset & (NBYTEPERWORD-1);
718                     assign total_flit       = last_byte_offset >> $clog2(NBYTEPERWORD);
719                     
720                     
721                     reg [7:0] flit_cnt;
722                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
723        1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
724        1/1                  flit_cnt <= #DLY 'd0;
725        1/1              end else if(rspo2rknp_xx_tail == 1'b1 && rknp_xx2rspo_ready == 1'b1) begin
726        1/1                  flit_cnt <= #DLY 'd0;
727        1/1              end else if(rspo2rknp_xx_valid == 1'b1 && rknp_xx2rspo_ready == 1'b1) begin
728        1/1                  flit_cnt <= #DLY flit_cnt + 1'b1;
729                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
730                     end
731                     
732                     
733                     wire [9*NBYTEPERWORD:0] spec_rsp_data;  //自组织特殊响应的body部分
734                     //------------------------------------------------------
735                     //   生成特殊响应be位序列
736                     //------------------------------------------------------
737                     reg [NBYTEPERWORD-1:0] be_temp;
738                     
739                     always @(*) begin
740        1/1              be_temp = {NBYTEPERWORD{1'b1}};
741                     
742                         // Mask the unused low lanes of the first flit.
743        1/1              if(flit_cnt == 'd0)
744        1/1                  be_temp = be_temp & ({NBYTEPERWORD{1'b1}} << first_valid_lane);
                        MISSING_ELSE
745                     
746                         // Mask the unused high lanes of the last flit. This is a separate if so
747                         // that a single-flit response receives both the head and tail masks.
748        1/1              if(flit_cnt == total_flit)
749        1/1                  be_temp = be_temp &
                        MISSING_ELSE
750                                       ({NBYTEPERWORD{1'b1}} >>
751                                        (NBYTEPERWORD - 1 - last_valid_lane));
752                     end
753                     //------------------------------------------------------
754                     //   1、填充byte位
755                     //   2、填充be位
756                     //------------------------------------------------------
757                     reg rspt2rspo_lw_d;
758                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
759        1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
760        1/1                  rspt2rspo_lw_d <= #DLY 1'b0;
761        1/1              end else if(rspt2rspo_valid == 1'b1 && rspt2rspo_lw == 1'b1 &&
762                                     rspo2rspt_ready == 1'b1)begin
763        1/1                  rspt2rspo_lw_d <= #DLY 1'b1;
764        1/1              end else if(rknp_xx2rspo_ready == 1'b1) begin
765        1/1                  rspt2rspo_lw_d <= #DLY 1'b0;
766                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
767                     end
768                     
769                     
770                     reg [9*NBYTEPERWORD:0] norm_rsp_data;  //由slave发回的响应的body部分
771                     localparam ADDR_OFFSET_WIDTH = 8 + $clog2(NBYTEPERWORD);
772                     
773                     
774                     // 计算头尾be有效位置，支持交织
775                     wire [$clog2(NBYTEPERWORD)-1:0] low_byte_disable_position;
776                     wire [$clog2(NBYTEPERWORD)-1:0] high_byte_disable_position;
777                     
778                     reg [$clog2(NBYTEPERWORD)-1:0] low_byte_disable_position_d;
779                     reg [$clog2(NBYTEPERWORD)-1:0] high_byte_disable_position_d;
780                     
781                     reg rhead_en_d;
782                     
783                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
784        1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
785        1/1                  rhead_en_d <= #DLY 1'b0;
786                         end else begin
787        1/1                  rhead_en_d <= #DLY rspo2reqo_rhead_en;
788                         end
789                     end
790                     
791                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
792        1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
793        1/1                  low_byte_disable_position_d <= #DLY 'd0;
794        1/1                  high_byte_disable_position_d <= #DLY 'd0;
795        1/1              end else if(rhead_en_d == 1'b1)begin
796        1/1                  low_byte_disable_position_d <= #DLY low_byte_disable_position;
797        1/1                  high_byte_disable_position_d <= #DLY high_byte_disable_position;
798                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
799                     end
800                     
801                     assign low_byte_disable_position = (rhead_en_d == 1'b1) ? reqo2rspo_rsp_addr[$clog2(NBYTEPERWORD)-1:0] : low_byte_disable_position_d;
802                     assign high_byte_disable_position = (rhead_en_d == 1'b1) ? reqo2rspo_rsp_addr[$clog2(NBYTEPERWORD)-1:0] + reqo2rspo_rsp_len : high_byte_disable_position_d;
803                     
804                     // A transaction can be split into several RKNP response packets by AXI read
805                     // interleaving. rspo2rknp_xx_head is asserted at the beginning of every such
806                     // packet, but only the first packet carries the original OK/ERR status. A
807                     // resumed packet carries CONT and must not apply the transaction's low-lane
808                     // mask again.
809                     wire norm_rsp_first_flit;
810                     wire norm_rsp_last_flit;
811                     reg  [NBYTEPERWORD-1:0] norm_be_temp;
812                     
813                     assign norm_rsp_first_flit = rspo2rknp_xx_valid &&
814                                                  rspo2rknp_xx_head &&
815                                                  (reqo2rspo_rsp_status != CONT);
816                     assign norm_rsp_last_flit  = rspo2rknp_xx_valid &&
817                                                  rspt2rspo_lw_d;
818                     
819                     // The first and last masks are deliberately two independent if statements.
820                     // A narrow read can fit in one flit, in which case HEAD and LW are both one
821                     // and both masks must be applied to the same BE vector.
822                     always @(*) begin
823        1/1              norm_be_temp = {NBYTEPERWORD{1'b1}};
824                     
825        1/1              if(norm_rsp_first_flit)
826        1/1                  norm_be_temp = norm_be_temp &
                        MISSING_ELSE
827                                            ({NBYTEPERWORD{1'b1}} <<
828                                             low_byte_disable_position);
829                     
830        1/1              if(norm_rsp_last_flit)
831        1/1                  norm_be_temp = norm_be_temp &
                        MISSING_ELSE
832                                            ({NBYTEPERWORD{1'b1}} >>
833                                             (NBYTEPERWORD - 1 -
834                                              high_byte_disable_position));
835                     end
836                     
837                     generate
838                         for(i=0; i<NBYTEPERWORD; i=i+1) begin
839                             always @(*) begin
840        8/8                      norm_rsp_data[i*9+1] = norm_be_temp[i];
841                             end
842                             always @(*) begin
843        8/8                      norm_rsp_data[i*9+9:i*9+2] = rsp_data[i*9+9:i*9+2];
844                             end
845                             assign spec_rsp_data[i*9+9:i*9+2] = 1'b0;
846                             // assign norm_rsp_data[i*9+9:i*9+2] = rsp_data[i*9+9:i*9+2];
847                     
848                             assign spec_rsp_data[i*9+1] = be_temp[i];  //特殊响应的be填充
849                             // assign norm_rsp_data[i*9+1] = () ? 1'b0 : 1'b1;  
850                     
851                             //不应该在这里写LW
852                             // if(i == NBYTEPERWORD-1) begin
853                             //     assign spec_rsp_data[0] = 1'b1; //特殊响应的LW填充
854                             //     assign norm_rsp_data[0] = 1'b1; //常规响应的LW填充
855                             // end else begin
856                             //     assign spec_rsp_data[0] = 1'b0;
857                             //     assign norm_rsp_data[0] = 1'b0;
858                             // end
859                     
860                         end
861                     endgenerate
862                     
863                     //合并到上面了
864                     // generate 
865                     //     for(i=0; i<NBYTEPERWORD; i=i+1) begin
866                     //         assign spec_rsp_data[i*9+9:i*9+2] = 1'b0;
867                     //         assign norm_rsp_data[i*9+9:i*9+2] = rsp_data[i*9-1:i*9-8];
868                     //     end
869                     // endgenerate
870                     
871                     
872                     
873                     //------------------------------------------------------
874                     //  索引值选择
875                     //------------------------------------------------------
876                     
877                     reg [INDEX_WITH-1:0] buff_index_d;
878                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
879        1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) 
880        1/1                  buff_index_d <= #DLY 'd0;
881                         else 
882        1/1                  buff_index_d <= #DLY buff_index;
883                     end
884                     
885                     always @(*) begin
886        1/1              if(spec_req_ready_en == 1'b1)
887        1/1                  buff_index = spec_req_ready_index;
888                         else
889        1/1                  buff_index = buff_index_d; //其他情况保持
890                     end
891                     
892                     //------------------------------------------------------
893                     //  锁存当前特殊响应的类型、tag索引、ErrorCode与Status
894                     //  spec_req_buffer会在rhead同拍释放，因此这些字段必须在释放前保存。
895                     //------------------------------------------------------
896                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
897        1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
898        1/1                  spec_rsp_errcode <= #DLY 3'd0;
899        1/1                  spec_rsp_status <= #DLY 2'd0;
900        1/1                  spec_rsp_opc <= #DLY 2'd0;
901        1/1                  spec_rsp_axid <= #DLY 'd0;
902        1/1                  spec_rsp_tag_name_index <= #DLY 'd0;
903        1/1              end else if(spec_dispatch_en == 1'b1) begin
904        1/1                  if(timout_fifo_rden == 1'b1) begin
905        1/1                      spec_rsp_errcode <= #DLY TIM_OUT;
906        1/1                      spec_rsp_status <= #DLY ERR;
907        1/1                      spec_rsp_opc <= #DLY wd2rspo_opc;
908        1/1                      spec_rsp_axid <= #DLY wd2rspo_axid;
909        1/1                      spec_rsp_tag_name_index <= #DLY wd_tag_name_index;
910                             end else begin
911        1/1                      spec_rsp_errcode <= #DLY
912                                     spec_req_buffer[buff_index][BUFF_ERRC_OFFSET +: 3];
913        1/1                      spec_rsp_status <= #DLY
914                                     spec_req_buffer[buff_index][BUFF_STAT_OFFSET +: 2];
915        1/1                      spec_rsp_opc <= #DLY
916                                     spec_req_buffer[buff_index][BUFF_OPC_OFFSET +: 2];
917        1/1                      spec_rsp_axid <= #DLY
918                                     spec_req_buffer[buff_index][BUFF_AXID_OFFSET +: AXID_WITH];
919        1/1                      spec_rsp_tag_name_index <= #DLY spec_tag_name_index;
920                             end
921                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
922                     end
923                     
924                     //------------------------------------------------------
925                     //  生成常规响应的status值
926                     //------------------------------------------------------
927                     
928                     
929                     
930                     // The request-side status describes the RKNP packet position: the first packet
931                     // is OK and a packet resumed after read interleaving is CONT.  The downstream
932                     // AXI response status describes whether the actual slave completion failed.
933                     // For the first packet, use the AXI-derived OK/ERR value captured together with
934                     // the response HEAD.  Preserve CONT for resumed packets so the existing RKNP
935                     // interleaving semantics are unchanged.
936                     wire [1:0] norm_rsp_status;
937                     assign norm_rsp_status = (reqo2rspo_rsp_status == CONT) ?
938                                              CONT : rsp_status;
939                     
940                     //------------------------------------------------------
941                     //  RKNP协议组包输出
942                     //------------------------------------------------------
943                     reg rsp_phase; // 为0表示响应通道空闲
944                     reg rsp_phase_temp; 
945                     reg rspt2rspo_lw_d2; // 用于捕捉rspt2rspo_lw_d的下降沿
946                     wire rspo2rknp_xx_lw_neg; // rspt2rspo_lw_d的下降沿
947                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
948        1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
949        1/1                  rspt2rspo_lw_d2 <= #DLY 1'b0;
950                         end else begin
951        1/1                  rspt2rspo_lw_d2 <= #DLY rspt2rspo_lw_d;
952                         end
953                     end
954                     
955                     assign rspo2rknp_xx_lw_neg = (~rspt2rspo_lw_d) & rspt2rspo_lw_d2;
956                     
957                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
958        1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
959        1/1                  rsp_phase_temp <= #DLY 1'b0;
960        1/1              end else if(rspo2rknp_xx_tail == 1'b1 &&
961                                     rknp_xx2rspo_ready == 1'b1) begin
962        1/1                  rsp_phase_temp <= #DLY 1'b0;
963        1/1              end else if(rspo2rknp_xx_valid == 1'b1 &&
964                                     rknp_xx2rspo_ready == 1'b1) begin
965        1/1                  rsp_phase_temp <= #DLY 1'b1;
966        1/1              end else if(rspt2rspo_lw_d == 1'b1) begin
967        1/1                  rsp_phase_temp <= #DLY 1'b0;
968                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
969                     end
970                     
971                     always @(*) begin
972        1/1              if(rspo2rknp_xx_lw_neg == 1'b1) begin
973        1/1                  rsp_phase = 1'b0;
974                         end else begin
975        1/1                  rsp_phase = rspo2rknp_xx_valid | rsp_phase_temp;
976                         end
977                     end
978                     
979                     // 特殊响应只能由一个无组合环的dispatch事件启动：
980                     // 1) 普通响应通道空闲；
981                     // 2) 当前响应尾拍握手，可以无气泡衔接下一笔特殊响应。
982                     assign rsp_tail_hs = rspo2rknp_xx_tail &&
983                                          rspo2rknp_xx_valid &&
984                                          rknp_xx2rspo_ready;
985                     
986                     // rsp_phase会覆盖普通多拍响应的拍间空隙，因此不能只用valid判断空闲。
987                     assign rsp_path_idle = (cur_state == NORM_RSP) &&
988                                            (rsp_phase == 1'b0);
989                     assign timeout_pending = ~timout_fifo_empty;
990                     assign dispatch_boundary = rsp_path_idle || rsp_tail_hs;
991                     
992                     assign spec_err_pending = spec_req_ready_en;
993                     assign spec_dispatch_en = dispatch_boundary &&
994                                               (spec_err_pending || timout_fifo_rden);
995                     
996                     
997                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
998        1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
999        1/1                  cur_state <= #DLY NORM_RSP;
1000                        end else begin
1001       1/1                  cur_state <= #DLY next_state;
1002                        end
1003                    end
1004                    
1005                    always @(*) begin
1006       1/1              case(cur_state)
1007                            NORM_RSP: begin
1008       1/1                      if(spec_dispatch_en == 1'b1)
1009       1/1                          next_state = SPEC_RSP;
1010                                else
1011       1/1                          next_state = NORM_RSP;
1012                            end
1013                            SPEC_RSP: begin
1014       1/1                      if(rsp_tail_hs == 1'b1 &&
1015                                   spec_dispatch_en == 1'b0)
1016       1/1                          next_state = NORM_RSP;
1017                                else
1018       1/1                          next_state = SPEC_RSP;
1019                            end
1020                            default:
1021       1/1                      next_state = NORM_RSP;
1022                        endcase
1023                    end
1024                    
1025                    
1026                    reg buff_rsp_flag_d;
1027                    always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
1028       1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
1029       1/1                  buff_rsp_flag_d <= #DLY 1'b0;
1030                        end else begin
1031       1/1                  buff_rsp_flag_d <= #DLY buff_rsp_flag;
1032                        end
1033                    end
1034                    
1035                    
1036                    
1037                    
1038                    always @(*) begin
1039       1/1              case(cur_state)
1040                            NORM_RSP: begin
1041       1/1                      if(rspo2rknp_xx_valid == 1'b1 && reqo2rspo_timout == 1'b0 && buff_rsp_flag_d == 1'b0) begin  //对常规响应，忽略超时响应与bufferable响应
1042                    
1043       1/1                          rspo2rknp_xx_data = {
1044                                        norm_rsp_data,
1045                                        rsp_errcode,
1046                                        reqo2rspo_rsp_user[AUSER_WITH+8], //RKNUSER
1047                                        rsp_user,                         //AxUSER
1048                                        reqo2rspo_rsp_user[7:0],          //AxLOCL\AxPORT\AxCACHE
1049                                        reqo2rspo_rsp_addr,
1050                                        norm_rsp_status,                  // AXI OK/ERR or RKNP CONT
1051                                        rsp_opc,
1052                                        reqo2rspo_rsp_ordkey,
1053                                        reqo2rspo_rsp_tid,
1054                                        reqo2rspo_rsp_iid,
1055                                        reqo2rspo_rsp_urg};
1056                                    
1057                                end else begin
1058       1/1                          rspo2rknp_xx_data = 'd0;
1059                                end
1060                            end
1061                            SPEC_RSP: begin
1062       1/1                      if(rspo2rknp_xx_valid == 1'b1) begin
1063       1/1                          rspo2rknp_xx_data = {
1064                                        spec_rsp_data,
1065                                        spec_rsp_errcode,
1066                                        reqo2rspo_rsp_user,
1067                                        reqo2rspo_rsp_addr,
1068                                        spec_rsp_status,
1069                                        spec_rsp_opc,
1070                                        reqo2rspo_rsp_ordkey,
1071                                        reqo2rspo_rsp_tid,
1072                                        reqo2rspo_rsp_iid,
1073                                        reqo2rspo_rsp_urg
1074                                        };
1075                                end
                        MISSING_ELSE
1076                            end
                        MISSING_DEFAULT
1077                        endcase
1078                    end
1079                    
1080                    
1081                    //------------------------------------------------------
1082                    //   1、传输rspo2wad_offset_addr
1083                    //   2、传输rspo2wad_axid
1084                    //------------------------------------------------------
1085                    assign rspo2wad_axid = rsp_axid;
1086                    assign rspo2wad_offset_addr = reqo2rspo_rsp_offset_addr;
1087                    
1088                    
1089                    //------------------------------------------------------
1090                    //   1、生成rspo2rknp_xx_head
1091                    //   2、生成rspo2rknp_xx_tail
1092                    //   3、生成rspo2rknp_xx_valid
1093                    //------------------------------------------------------
1094                    reg rspt2rspo_head_d;
1095                    reg rspt2rspo_tail_d;
1096                    reg rspt2rspo_valid_d;
1097                    
1098                    always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
1099       1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
1100       1/1                  rspt2rspo_head_d <= #DLY 1'b0;
1101       1/1              end else if(rspt2rspo_head == 1'b1 && rspt2rspo_valid == 1'b1 &&
1102                                    rspo2rspt_ready == 1'b1) begin
1103       1/1                  rspt2rspo_head_d <= #DLY 1'b1;
1104       1/1              end else if(rknp_xx2rspo_ready == 1'b1) begin
1105       1/1                  rspt2rspo_head_d <= #DLY 1'b0;
1106                        end
                        MISSING_ELSE
1107                    end
1108                    always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
1109       1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
1110       1/1                  rspt2rspo_tail_d <= #DLY 1'b0;
1111       1/1              end else if(rspt2rspo_tail == 1'b1 && rspt2rspo_valid == 1'b1 &&
1112                                    rspo2rspt_ready == 1'b1) begin
1113       1/1                  rspt2rspo_tail_d <= #DLY 1'b1;
1114       1/1              end else if(rknp_xx2rspo_ready == 1'b1) begin
1115       1/1                  rspt2rspo_tail_d <= #DLY 1'b0;
1116                        end
                        MISSING_ELSE
1117                    end
1118                    always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
1119       1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
1120       1/1                  rspt2rspo_valid_d <= #DLY 1'b0;
1121       1/1              end else if(rspt2rspo_valid == 1'b1 && rspo2rspt_ready == 1'b1) begin
1122       1/1                  rspt2rspo_valid_d <= #DLY 1'b1;
1123       1/1              end else if(rknp_xx2rspo_ready == 1'b1) begin
1124       1/1                  rspt2rspo_valid_d <= #DLY 1'b0;
1125                        end
                        MISSING_ELSE
1126                    end
1127                    
1128                    reg spec_rsp_phase; // 特殊响应恢复区间
1129                    always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
1130       1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
1131       1/1                  spec_rsp_phase <= #DLY 1'b0;
1132       1/1              end else if(spec_dispatch_en == 1'b1) begin
1133                            // dispatch同时完成head读取、属性锁存和自组包启动。
1134       1/1                  spec_rsp_phase <= #DLY 1'b1;
1135       1/1              end else if(rsp_tail_hs == 1'b1) begin
1136       1/1                  spec_rsp_phase <= #DLY 1'b0;
1137                        end
                        MISSING_ELSE
1138                    end
1139                    
1140                    
1141                    always @(*) begin
1142       1/1              case(cur_state)
1143                            NORM_RSP: begin
1144                                // if(reqo2rspo_timout == 1'b0 && reqo2rspo_rsp_status != CONT && rsp_opc == WR) begin //若该响应为已经超时响应或early response的real response，则不传输
1145       1/1                      if(reqo2rspo_timout == 1'b0 && buff_rsp_flag_d == 1'b0) begin //若该响应为已经超时响应或early response的real response，则不传输
1146       1/1                          rspo2rknp_xx_head = rspt2rspo_head_d;
1147       1/1                          rspo2rknp_xx_tail = rspt2rspo_tail_d;
1148       1/1                          rspo2rknp_xx_valid = rspt2rspo_valid_d;
1149                                end else begin
1150       1/1                          rspo2rknp_xx_head = 1'b0;
1151       1/1                          rspo2rknp_xx_tail = 1'b0;
1152       1/1                          rspo2rknp_xx_valid = 1'b0;
1153                                end
1154                            end
1155                            SPEC_RSP: begin
1156       1/1                      if(spec_rsp_phase == 1'b1) begin
1157       1/1                          if(rspo2rknp_xx_data[RSP_OPC_OFFSET +: 2] == RD) begin
1158       1/1                              if(flit_cnt == 'd0) begin
1159       1/1                                  rspo2rknp_xx_head = 1'b1;
1160       1/1                                  rspo2rknp_xx_tail = (total_flit == 'd0);
1161       1/1                                  rspo2rknp_xx_valid = 1'b1;
1162       1/1                              end else if(flit_cnt == total_flit)begin
1163       1/1                                  rspo2rknp_xx_head = 1'b0;
1164       1/1                                  rspo2rknp_xx_tail = 1'b1;
1165       1/1                                  rspo2rknp_xx_valid = 1'b1;
1166                                        end else begin
1167       1/1                                  rspo2rknp_xx_head = 1'b0;
1168       1/1                                  rspo2rknp_xx_tail = 1'b0;
1169       1/1                                  rspo2rknp_xx_valid = 1'b1;
1170                                        end
1171                                    end else begin
1172       1/1                              rspo2rknp_xx_head = 1'b1;
1173       1/1                              rspo2rknp_xx_tail = 1'b1;

                        MISSING_DEFAULT

1231                                        rspo2reqo_rhead_en = 1'b1;

1236                                                [BUFF_TAGCNT_OFFSET +:

1239                                        del_head_en = 1'b1;
1240                                    end
1241                                end else if(cur_state == NORM_RSP) begin
1242                                    rspo2reqo_head_index = norm_rsp_head_index;
1243                                    rspo2reqo_rhead_en =
1244                                        rspt2rspo_head && rspt2rspo_valid &&
1245                                        rspo2rspt_ready;

1247                                        rspt2rspo_lw && rspt2rspo_valid &&

1250                            end

1257

1276                                    end

1279       1/1                              rspo2reqo_head_index = norm_rsp_head_index;

1295                                end
                        MISSING_ELSE
1296                            end

1301                    
1302                    always @(*) begin

1304                        // level copy of the registered RKNP output valid.  req_order uses it to

1276                       end

                                -4-
